### [183] [Uncertainty-Aware Transfer Learning for Cross-Building Energy Forecasting: Toward Robust and Scalable District-Level Energy Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29733)
*Shadmehr Zaregarizi,Khashayar Yavari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT）的不确定性感知迁移学习框架，用于跨建筑能耗预测。在丹麦奥尔堡大学（源域）与瑞士EMPA NEST建筑（目标域）上进行评估，引入迁移鲁棒性指数（TRI）量化泛化质量。通过四策略层冻结消融实验发现，仅微调455个输出层参数（Probe-Only）优于全微调（TRI=3,097）。Monte Carlo Dropout实现93.2%的预测区间覆盖概率。数据分析表明目标域数据量增加可单调提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将数据驱动的能耗预测扩展到区域级别，需要能够在跨建筑场景中复用、仅需少量目标域数据且具有可靠不确定性估计的模型。

Method: 基于Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT)构建不确定性感知迁移学习框架；提出迁移鲁棒性指数（TRI）作为架构无关的泛化质量度量；采用四策略层冻结微调消融实验（其中Probe-Only仅更新806K参数中的455个输出层参数）；使用Monte Carlo Dropout进行不确定性量化。

Result: Probe-Only微调取得最佳迁移质量（TRI=3,097），优于全微调；Monte Carlo Dropout预测区间覆盖概率达93.2%，接近95%名义目标；数据匮乏分析显示性能随目标域数据量单调提升。

Conclusion: TFT编码器学习到了可迁移的时序表示，仅微调输出层即可实现高效跨建筑迁移，为区域级能耗部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Scaling data-driven energy forecasting to district level requires models that can be re-used across buildings with minimal target-domain data and honest uncertainty estimates. We present an uncertainty-aware transfer learning (TL) framework for cross-building energy forecasting based on the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT), evaluated on a newly released high-resolution real sub-meter dataset: an educational building at Aalborg University, Denmark (source) and the multi-typology NEST building at EMPA, Switzerland (target). We introduce the Transfer Robustness Index (TRI), an architecture-agnostic metric for quantifying generalization quality across domain gaps. A four-strategy layer-freezing ablation shows that Probe-Only fine-tuning, updating only 455 output-layer parameters out of 806K, achieves the best transfer quality (TRI = 3,097), outperforming full fine-tuning and suggesting that TFT encoders learn transferable temporal representations. Monte Carlo Dropout yields a prediction interval coverage probability of 93.2%, close to the nominal 95% target. A data-scarcity analysis further shows monotonic improvement with increasing target-domain data, providing practical guidance for district energy deployment.

</details>


### [184] [Citation-Closure Retrieval and Per-Rule Attribution for Real-World Regulatory Compliance Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29742)
*Yeong-Joon Ju,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向法规合规QA的基准RegOps-Bench和统一框架RefWalk，通过操作知识图谱和主题锚点驱动的跨文档引用遍历、多视图融合与逐规则归因，显著提升了检索召回率和引用准确性，并揭示了现有系统在扁平结构规则上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在法规合规场景中面临引用边扁平化、检索扩展碎片化和事后归因脆弱等问题，无法满足多层权威结构下严格的溯源需求，亟需专门化的基准和方法。

Method: 提出RefWalk框架，核心包括：(1) 构建操作知识图谱（Operational Knowledge Graph）以结构化表示层级法规；(2) 共享主题锚点驱动跨文档引用遍历；(3) 基于最大聚合的多视角候选融合；(4) 逐规则归因机制，将模型输出显式映射到源文档。

Result: 在RegOps-Bench上建立强基线，检索召回率和引用准确性均有显著提升；在HIPAA对比评估中，现有系统在扁平结构规则上表现饱和，验证了RegOps-Bench的必要性。

Conclusion: RegOps-Bench和RefWalk有效解决了法规合规场景下的结构化检索与归因挑战，揭示了传统方法在层级规则上的不足，为后续研究提供了基准和框架。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) for regulatory compliance demands rigorous traceability via comprehensive citations across multi-tiered authority structures. Unlike traditional multi-hop or legal QA, this task requires structured procedural lookups and evidence-set closure rather than entity resolution or case-law reasoning. Existing RAG systems struggle here due to flattened citation edges, fragmented retrieval expansions, and fragile post-hoc attribution. We formalize Regulatory Compliance QA with RegOps-Bench, a novel benchmark featuring an Operational Knowledge Graph derived from complex national R\&D regulations. To address these bottlenecks, we propose RefWalk, a unified framework driven by a shared topic anchor. RefWalk traverses cross-document citations, fuses multi-view candidates via max-based aggregation, and enforces per-rule attribution to explicitly map claims to sources. We establish a strong baseline with substantial improvements in retrieval recall and citation accuracy. Finally, a contrastive evaluation on a U.S. health compliance dataset (HIPAA) reveals that existing systems exhibit saturation on flat-structure rules, underscoring the need for RegOps-Bench. Our code is available at https://github.com/yeongjoonJu/RefWalk.

</details>


### [185] [Why Specialist Models Still Matter: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Paradigm for Medical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29744)
*Yanan Wang,Shuaicong Hu,Jian Liu,Guohui Zhou,Aiguo Wang,Cuiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HetMedAgent是一个异构医学多智能体框架，通过协调通用大语言模型、领域专用模型和临床医生，在三个临床决策任务中显著优于单一模型，证明了专用模型的不可替代价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨通用LLM（如GPT、Claude）在医疗领域表现出色的背景下，领域专用医学模型是否会被淘汰的问题，提出未来方向不是构建单一医学基础模型，而是多智能体协作。

Method: 提出HetMedAgent异构医学多智能体框架，包含冲突感知证据融合、基于不确定性的临床医生干预触发和自适应阈值校准三大机制。

Result: 在三个真实临床决策任务中，通用LLM与领域专用模型的协同显著优于单独使用任一类模型，验证了专用模型在模态特定分析中的不可替代价值。

Conclusion: 医学AI的未来应从构建医学LLM或基础模型转向多智能体协作，在通用推理能力与领域专用精度之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The impressive performance of generalist large language models (LLMs) such as GPT and Claude in healthcare raises a critical question: will domain-specific medical specialist models become obsolete? We argue that the future of medical artificial intelligence (AI) lies not in building monolithic medical foundation models, nor in replacing human expertise, but in orchestrating collaboration among generalist LLMs, domain-specific specialist models, and clinicians. We propose HetMedAgent, a heterogeneous medical multi-agent framework that enables conflict-aware evidence fusion, uncertainty-based clinician intervention triggering, and adaptive threshold calibration. Experiments on three real-world clinical decision-making tasks demonstrate that the synergy between generalist LLMs and domain-specific specialist models significantly outperforms using either type of model alone, validating the irreplaceable value of specialist models in modality-specific analysis. HetMedAgent represents a shift from building medical LLMs or foundation models to multi-agent collaboration, achieving a balance between general reasoning capabilities and domain-specific precision.

</details>


### [186] [LFQ: Logit-aware Final-block Quantization for Boosting the Generation Quality of Low-Bit Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29756)
*Jung Hyun Lee,June Yong Yang,Jungwook Choi,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型低比特量化在生成任务中性能下降的问题，本文提出LFQ方法，通过最小化FP模型与量化模型在最后Transformer块的logits交叉熵，有效提升了生成任务准确率，同时保持语言建模和理解任务性能不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块级PTQ方法在基础语言建模和理解任务上表现良好，但在生成任务（尤其是长回复和长思维链）中质量下降，影响了任务准确率。作者将此归因于两个因素：(i) 块级优化忽略了去嵌入层（LM head）；(ii) 依赖MSE损失目标。两者导致量化模型的token概率分布与FP模型不一致。

Method: 提出Logit-aware Final-block Quantization (LFQ)，作为块级PTQ的简单有效增强方案：在量化最后一个Transformer块时，最小化FP模型与量化模型之间logits的交叉熵，从而在logit层面对齐token概率分布。

Result: LFQ在多种模型族上，相较于最先进的块级PTQ方法，一致地提升了复杂生成任务的准确率，同时在语言建模和理解任务上保持与FP基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 通过针对最后一个Transformer块采用交叉熵损失而非MSE，并考虑LM head的影响，LFQ有效解决了块级PTQ在生成任务中的概率分布失配问题，是一种简单且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: As large language models continue to scale, low-bit weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) offers a practical solution to their memory-efficient deployment. Although block-wise PTQ is capable of matching the full-precision (FP) baseline on basic language modeling and understanding, its quality is degraded for generative tasks -- especially at longer responses and extended chains of thought, which is critical in boosting task accuracy. We attribute this shortfall to two factors: (i) the omission of the unembedding layer (the LM head) in block-wise optimization and (ii) the reliance on the mean squared error (MSE) objective. Both factors cause the token probability distribution of the quantized model to misalign with that of the FP model, yielding notable accuracy drops on text generation benchmarks. To rectify the discrepancy, we introduce Logit-aware Final-block Quantization (LFQ), a simple yet effective enhancement to block-wise PTQ that quantizes the final Transformer block by minimizing the cross-entropy between the logits of the FP model and those of its quantized counterpart. By aligning token probabilities at the logit level in the final block, LFQ consistently improves the accuracy of complex generation tasks over state-of-the-art block-wise PTQ across diverse model families, while maintaining parity with FP baselines on language modeling and understanding.

</details>


### [187] [Croissant Tasks: A Metadata Format for Reproducible Machine Learning Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29786)
*Omar Benjelloun,Leonardo Martins Bianco,Isabelle Guyon,Thanh Gia Hieu Khuong,Jonathan Lebensold,Sebastian Lobentanzer,Luis Oala,Benedictus Kent Rachmat,Ihsan Ullah,Peyman Vahidi,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Croissant Tasks，一种声明式、机器可执行的元数据格式，将底层实现细节抽象为高层规范，实现概念可重复性（通过智能体独立生成实现来验证主张），包含规范定义、自动化LLM流水线将现有基准转换为此格式，以及实证验证自主智能体能据此生成功能完整的复现流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中的可重复性面临严峻挑战，根本原因包括执行细节说明不充分以及软件环境脆弱。现有以人为中心的解决方案（如检查清单和手动验证）虽然有效但劳动密集、难以规模化。

Method: (1) 提出Croissant Tasks规范，形式化解耦任务问题与解决方案；(2) 设计自动化LLM流水线，将现有基准测试转换为此格式；(3) 通过自主智能体实证验证该格式的有效性。

Result: 自主智能体能够摄入这些规范，从零开始生成功能完整、准确的可复现流水线。

Conclusion: 该格式可作为机器学习中自动化和概念可重复性的新基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility is fundamental to the scientific method, yet remains a critical challenge in machine learning. Contributing factors include underspecified execution details and brittle software environments. Human-centric remedies, such as checklists and manual verification, help but require intensive effort and fail to scale. To address this, we introduce Croissant Tasks: a declarative, machine-actionable metadata format that abstracts low-level implementation details into high-level specifications. This format enables conceptual reproducibility: verifying claims via independent, agent-generated implementations rather than brittle source code replication. We contribute: (1) the Croissant Tasks specification, formally decoupling task problem from solution; (2) an automated LLM pipeline that retrofits existing benchmarks into this format; and (3) empirical validation showing autonomous agents can ingest these specifications to generate functional, accurate reproduction pipelines from scratch. We envision this format as a new foundation for automated and conceptual reproducibility in machine learning.

</details>


### [188] [Certified Policy Optimisation for Nested Causal Bandits via PAC-Bayes Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29788)
*Tim Woydt,Paul-David Zuercher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌套上下文因果bandit（NCCB）框架建模跨时间尺度的因果耦合决策，并设计嵌套因果汤普森采样（NCTS）算法，通过机制分解的后验采样和递归行动实现层次化决策。理论贡献是因果PAC-Bayesian超额风险界，可从历史数据离线、任意时刻认证任意部署策略。实验表明，因子化SCM机制后验在分布外零样本迁移上优于联合回归，递归元到内层commit显著优于联合commit，且认证界限随离线数据积累收缩。结合这些结果，建立了渐进式认证移交的安全部署方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准bandit和强化学习理论无法捕捉不同时间尺度决策之间的因果耦合——战略决策因果性地塑造了后续策略选择发生的上下文环境。现有方法缺少对此类跨尺度因果关系的形式化建模与理论分析。

Method: 形式化定义嵌套上下文因果bandit（NCCB）为层次化SCM，每层动作设定下一层的上下文分布；提出嵌套因果汤普森采样（NCTS），每轮抽取一个机制因子化的后验并在其下递归行动；导出因果PAC-Bayesian超额风险界，实现离线、任意时刻的部署策略认证。

Result: 实验显示：(1) 因子化SCM机制后验在分布外零样本迁移显著优于RFF-GP联合回归；(2) 递归元到内层commit在分布内显著优于联合commit；(3) 认证界限随离线数据积累显著收缩。

Conclusion: 提出渐进式认证移交安全部署方法：每个时间尺度可在独立认证增益后，从传统控制器切换至NCTS，实现分层的安全部署。

Abstract: Critical sequential decisions are rarely single-timescale: a strategic decision causally shapes the context in which every subsequent tactical choice is made; standard bandit and reinforcement-learning theory does not capture this causal coupling between timescales. We formalise the problem class as Nested Contextual Causal Bandits (NCCBs), a hierarchical SCM where each level's action sets the next level's context distribution, and propose Nested Causal Thompson Sampling (NCTS), which draws one mechanism-factorised belief per episode and acts recursively under it. Our main theoretical result is a causal PAC-Bayesian excess-risk bound that certifies any candidate deployment policy from historic data alone, off-policy and anytime, answering the deployment question: can we trust this agent here, and at what risk? Experiments on a hierarchical SCM show that, against a matched RFF-GP joint regression on the same function class, the factorised SCM-mechanism posterior transfers significantly better zero-shot under exogenous distribution shifts, the recursive meta-to-inner commit significantly dominates the joint-commit alternative in distribution, and the certificate significantly contracts as offline data accumulates. Combining these results, we establish progressive certified handover, a safe-deployment method: each timescale flips from a legacy controller to NCTS when gains can be certified, independently of the others.

</details>


### [189] [SkillsInjector: Dynamic Skill Context Construction for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29794)
*Yanchao Li,Wanhao Liu,Ben Gao,Jiaqing Xie,Zhehong Ai,Na Zou,Yuqiang Li,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillsInjector，一种两阶段自适应方法，通过上下文规划器学习技能偏好并自适应调整技能数量，结合集合感知渲染器优化技能描述呈现方式，在三个基准上分别超过最强基线3.9、6.1和7.3个百分点，证明技能注入的静态处理会损害性能，而优化注入上下文本身能显著提升智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将技能注入视为静态步骤，使用固定标准选择技能、预先设定预算、保持描述不变，这种静态处理会削弱技能效用，因为技能的选择、数量和呈现方式都会影响下游性能。

Method: 提出两阶段自适应方法SkillsInjector：第一阶段，上下文规划器学习执行层面的技能偏好，为每个任务自适应调整技能数量；第二阶段，集合感知渲染器根据共同注入的邻居技能，定制化调整所选技能描述的呈现方式。

Result: 在tau2-bench、SkillsBench和ALFWorld三个基准上，SkillsInjector均取得最高分数，分别超过最强基线3.9、6.1和7.3个百分点。消融实验表明，技能选择、自适应预算和集合感知渲染各自都对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 技能增强型智能体受益于对注入上下文本身的优化，而非仅仅增加技能数量。静态注入方式可能适得其反，自适应地决定暴露哪些技能、包含多少技能以及如何呈现技能描述至关重要。

Abstract: LLM agents now draw on growing skill libraries to handle complex tasks. However, injecting more skills does not always improve task completion and can even degrade it. Existing methods still treat skill injection as a static step, selecting skills with fixed criteria, fixing the budget in advance, and leaving descriptions unchanged. We argue that this static treatment can undermine the utility of skills, because which skills are exposed, how many are included, and how they are presented all affect downstream performance. We propose SkillsInjector, a two-stage adaptive method that jointly addresses these decisions. First, a context planner learns execution-grounded skill preferences and admits an adaptive number of skills for each task. A set-aware renderer then tailors how selected descriptions are presented relative to their co-injected neighbors. Across tau2-bench, SkillsBench, and ALFWorld, SkillsInjector achieves the highest score, improving over the strongest baseline by 3.9, 6.1, and 7.3 percentage points, respectively. Ablation studies show that skill selection, adaptive budgeting, and set-aware rendering each contribute to the gain. These results show that skill-augmented agents benefit from optimizing the injected context itself. Code will be released upon publication

</details>


### [190] [SAAS: Self-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Over-Search Mitigation in Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29796)
*Yunbo Tang,Chengyi Yang,Shiyu Liu,Zhishang Xiang,Zerui Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAAS是一个强化学习框架，通过动态自我感知机制精准调控LLM智能体的搜索行为，在保持准确性的同时显著减少过度搜索问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic搜索系统缺乏自我认知能力，无法识别自身知识边界，导致盲目触发搜索以及即使已收集足够证据仍继续搜索的过度搜索问题，造成严重的推理延迟和计算成本。

Method: 提出三个关键组件：(i) 搜索边界建模机制——通过对比禁用搜索与启用搜索的轨迹来识别当前策略下的搜索边界；(ii) 边界感知奖励模块——将边界意识转化为轨迹级惩罚，抑制不必要和冗余的搜索；(iii) 分阶段优化策略——利用顺序课程优先推理再正则化搜索，避免奖励破解。

Result: 大量实验表明SAAS在保持准确性的同时显著减少了过度搜索。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习培养动态自我意识可以精准调控LLM智能体的搜索行为，有效解决过度搜索问题，实现效率与准确性的平衡。

Abstract: Agentic search enables LLMs to solve complex multi-hop questions through iterative reasoning and external search. Despite the effectiveness, these systems often suffer from a critical limitation in practice: agents fail to recognize their own knowledge boundaries, blindly triggering searches when internal knowledge suffices and failing to terminate search even when adequate evidence has been collected. The lack of self-awareness leads to severe \textbf{over-search}, incurring substantial inference latency and prohibitive computational cost. To this end, we propose SAAS, a novel RL framework designed to cultivate dynamic self-awareness that precisely regulates search behavior without compromising accuracy. SAAS introduces three key components: (i) a search boundary modeling mechanism, which identifies the search boundary under the evolving policy by contrasting search-disabled and search-enabled rollouts; (ii) a boundary-aware reward module, which translates this boundary awareness into trajectory-level penalties, suppressing unnecessary and redundant searches; and (iii) a stage-wise optimization strategy, which leverages a sequential curriculum to prioritize reasoning over search regularization, thereby avoiding reward hacking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAAS substantially reduces over-search, while maintaining accuracy. Our code is anonymously released at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/SAAS.

</details>


### [191] [AgentDoG 1.5: A Lightweight and Scalable Alignment Framework for AI Agent Safety and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29801)
*Dongrui Liu,Yu Li,Zhonghao Yang,Peng Wang,Guanxu Chen,Yuejin Xie,Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Yanxu Zhu,Tianyi Zhou,Leitao Yuan,Zhijie Zheng,Qihao Lin,Yimin Wang,Haoyu Luo,Shuai Shao,Chen Qian,Qingyu Liu,Ling Tang,Ruiyang Qin,Qihan Ren,Junxiao Yang,Kun Wang,Zhiheng Xi,Linfeng Zhang,Ranjie Duan,Bo Zhang,Wenjie Wang,Wen Shen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Yan Teng,Chaochao Lu,Rui Mei,Man Li,Jialing Tao,Xi Lin,Tianhang Zheng,Yong Liu,Quanshi Zhang,Lei Zhu,Xingjun Ma,Junhua Liu,Hui Xue,Xiaoxiang Zuo,Xiangnan He,Chao Shen,Xianglong Liu,Minlie Huang,Jing Shao,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentDoG 1.5，一个轻量可扩展的智能体安全对齐框架，通过更新安全分类体系、构建基于影响函数纯化的数据引擎，仅用约1k样本训练0.8B-8B参数模型，在复杂交互式智能体场景中达到与GPT-5.4相当的SOTA性能，并将Docker级部署开销降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代开放世界智能体（如OpenClaw）具备强大的跨环境执行能力，但引入了广泛的新安全风险源；同时前沿AI模型大幅降低了攻击门槛，现有智能体对齐框架不足以应对真实世界部署的安全威胁。

Method: 1) 更新智能体安全分类体系以覆盖Codex和OpenClaw执行场景的新兴风险；2) 构建基于影响函数纯化的分类学引导数据引擎，仅用约1k样本训练AgentDoG 1.5变体（0.8B, 2B, 4B, 8B）；3) 基于AgentDoG 1.5构建高效的智能体安全SFT和RL训练环境，将Docker级部署开销降低两个数量级；4) 部署为无需训练的在线护栏实现实时安全审核。

Result: AgentDoG 1.5在多样化和复杂的交互式智能体场景中达到SOTA性能，与GPT-5.4等领先闭源模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 本文提出的轻量框架有效解决了开放世界智能体的安全对齐问题，所有模型和数据集均已开源发布。

Abstract: Modern open-world agents such as OpenClaw exhibit powerful cross-environment execution capabilities yet introduce broad new safety risk sources. Meanwhile, advanced frontier AI models drastically lower attack barriers, rendering current agent alignment frameworks inadequate for real-world deployment. To tackle these emerging threats, we propose a lightweight and scalable agent safety alignment framework. Specifically, we update the agent safety taxonomy to accommodate emergent risks from Codex and OpenClaw execution scenarios. We further build a taxonomy-guided data engine with influence-function purification to train lightweight AgentDoG 1.5 variants (0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 8B parameters) using only around 1k samples, achieving comparable performance with leading closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4). Based on AgentDoG 1.5, we construct a highly efficient agentic safety SFT and RL training environment, which reduces deployment overhead in Docker-level environments by two orders of magnitude. Finally, we deploy AgentDoG 1.5 as a training-free online guardrail for real-time safety moderation. Extensive experimental results indicate that AgentDoG 1.5 achieves state-of-the-art performance in diverse and complex interactive agentic scenarios. All models and datasets are openly released.

</details>


### [192] [PRAIB: Peer Review AI Benchmark of Behaviour of LLM-Assisted Reviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29815)
*Krzysztof Żurawicki,Julia Farganus,Arkadiusz Gaweł,Mateusz Bystroński,Tomasz Jan Kajdanowicz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了同行评审AI基准测试（PRAIB），用于评估LLM生成的评审与人类评审之间的差异。通过对11,000条机器评审的分析发现，LLM的评分变异性更小、存在正向偏差且过度自信，生成的评审更长更复杂，但常忽略人类发现的细粒度缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着论文提交数量不断增加，LLM被用于辅助同行评审过程以提升速度和可扩展性，但尚不清楚LLM是否真正理解科学稿件还是仅生成看似评审的文本。作者旨在量化LLM与人类评审行为之间的差异。

Method: 构建PRAIB框架，定义评审特异性、风格和行为参与度等指标；使用5种闭源和开源模型对1,000篇ICLR/NeurIPS论文生成11,000条评审，覆盖2021-2025年，与原始人类反馈进行对比。

Result: LLM评分变异性更小、正向偏差、过度自信；交叉引用模式依赖于模型且与人类不同；LLM评审更长更复杂但常忽略人类标注的原子级弱点。

Conclusion: PRAIB可作为诊断工具，帮助识别LLM目前在同行评审中可可靠支持的方面以及需要进一步发展的方面。

Abstract: The growing number of submitted papers has motivated the exploration of Large Language Models (LLMs) as a means to support and augment the peer review process, particularly in terms of improving its speed and scalability. Yet, it remains unknown whether LLMs engage with scientific manuscripts in the same manner as human reviewers, or whether they merely produce review-looking text. To address this, we introduce the Peer Review AI Benchmark (PRAIB), a novel framework comprising thoroughly defined metrics that measure review specificity, style, and behavior of engagement. To complement the PRAIB framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study leveraging a dataset of 11,000 reviews generated by five proprietary and open-source models for 1,000 ICLR and NeurIPS papers. Spanning the 2021--2025 period, these machine-generated reviews are compared against original human feedback across diverse prompting strategies to identify systematic behavioral divergences. Our analysis reveals that the generated reviews diverge significantly from feedback provided by human reviewers: LLM ratings are less variable, positively biased, and overconfident, and their cross-reference patterns are model-dependent and distinct from human norms. Furthermore, when evaluated through PRAIB, we observe that LLMs tend to generate longer, more complex reviews, yet frequently overlook the atomic weaknesses noted by human reviewers. By characterizing where and how LLMs reviewing behavior departs from human norms, PRAIB provides the community with a diagnostic tool for identifying which aspects of the review process LLMs can reliably support today and which require further development before deployment.

</details>


### [193] [OptSkills: Learning Generalizable Optimization Skills from Problem Archetypes via Cluster-Based Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29829)
*Haochen Yang,Ke Zhao,Mengyuan Ma,Xingyu Lu,Xiangfeng Wang,Hong Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OptSkills是一个基于原型（archetype）中心技能学习与推理的智能体系统，用于自动化优化建模与求解，通过在问题聚类、建模范式探索、技能蒸馏和动态扩展等机制上创新，显著提升了分布内和分布外泛化能力，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动优化方法泛化能力有限：对表面叙事变化敏感，经验复用停留在案例层面，难以适应问题类型的偏移或涌现。

Method: 1) 按底层原型（archetype）而非表面叙事对问题聚类；2) 在每个聚类内探索多样化建模范式和求解器配置，将成功轨迹蒸馏为可复用的工作流级技能；3) 利用新获得的轨迹优化现有技能或扩展技能库。

Result: 在多样化问题类型数据集上微平均准确率达68.27%（SOTA）；在MIPLIB-NL上达26.91%，超越DeepSeek-V3.2-Thinking 4.53%；在Nano-CO上进行技能学习后，OOD NLCO基准上达72.79%。

Conclusion: OptSkills通过原型聚类与工作流级技能学习有效提升了自动化优化问题的泛化能力，在多个基准上取得了最优结果。

Abstract: Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically formulate and solve optimization problems from natural language has emerged as an efficient paradigm for automated optimization. However, existing methods still exhibit limited generalization: they are sensitive to superficial narrative variations, reuse experience mainly at the case level, and struggle to adapt to shifted or emerging problem types. We propose OptSkills, an archetype-centric skill learning and reasoning agent system for optimization modeling and solving. To improve robust generalization, our system clusters problems by their underlying archetypes rather than surface narratives. To improve in-distribution generalization, it explores diverse modeling paradigms and solver configurations within each cluster, then distills successful trajectories into reusable workflow-level skills. To improve out-of-distribution generalization, it refines existing skills or expands the skill library using newly obtained trajectories. Our system achieves a state-of-the-art micro-averaged accuracy of 68.27% on datasets encompassing diverse problem types and scenarios. In addition, on MIPLIB-NL, a highly challenging large-scale and high-dimensional benchmark, it achieves 26.91% accuracy, outperforming DeepSeek-V3.2-Thinking by 4.53%. After skill learning on Nano-CO, it reaches 72.79% on the OOD NLCO benchmark. Code and skills are available at https://github.com/fujiwaranoM0kou/OptSkills.

</details>


### [194] [OmniMatBench: A Human-Calibrated Multimodal Reasoning Benchmark Across 19 Materials Science Subfields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29833)
*Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Qian Tan,Weida Wang,Jue Wang,Ran Sun,Zhuo Yang,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Tianfan Fu,Lu Chen,Xin Chen,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniMatBench是一个由专家精心策划的多模态材料科学推理基准，包含3,171个问题，覆盖19个子领域。评估13个MLLM后发现最佳模型仅得0.372分，揭示当前模型在材料科学推理上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料学基准主要集中在属性预测、知识问答或表征理解等单一任务，缺乏从材料知识到应用的完整推理过程评估，因此亟需一个涵盖更广泛推理能力的多模态基准。

Method: 构建包含3,171个专家策划的QA和计算问题的基准，覆盖19个材料学子领域，分为四大类（基础知识、结构/工程材料、加工与制造、功能与应用材料），并评估13个开源和闭源多模态大语言模型（MLLM）。

Result: 最佳模型整体得分仅0.372；模型在不同子领域表现差异大，存在固定推理启发式、材料知识不均衡、在公式/检索/代码辅助下高级知识应用能力有限等问题。

Conclusion: OmniMatBench揭示了当前MLLM在材料科学推理方面的显著能力与局限，为构建可靠的AI助手奠定基础。

Abstract: As multimodal language models play an increasingly important role in scientific research, materials science offers a critical testbed due to its interdisciplinary, multimodal, and application-driven nature. However, existing materials benchmarks mainly focus on property prediction, knowledge QA, or characterization understanding, leaving the broader reasoning process from materials knowledge to application underexplored. To fill this gap, we present OmniMatBench, a human-calibrated multimodal reasoning benchmark for materials science. OmniMatBench contains 3,171 expert-curated QA and calculation problems across 19 materials-science subfields, spanning fundamental materials knowledge, structural and engineering materials, materials processing and manufacturing, and functional and applied materials. We evaluate 13 open-source and closed-source MLLMs and find that the best model achieves only a 0.372 overall score, revealing a substantial gap in current materials-science reasoning. Further analysis shows strong variation across subfields, fixed reasoning heuristics, uneven materials knowledge, and limited high-level knowledge application under formula-, retrieval-, and code-assisted settings. OmniMatBench provides crucial insights into the capabilities and limitations of current MLLMs and establishes a foundation for reliable AI assistants in materials-science research.

</details>


### [195] [Moment-KV: Momentum-Based Decode-Time KV Cache Compression for Long Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29873)
*Soumyadeep Jana,Sagar Nishad,Sanasam Ranbir Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Moment-KV 提出基于动量驱动的时间注意力聚合的 KV 缓存压缩方法，在解码阶段动态评估 token 重要性，避免了预填充缓存的压缩，相比现有方法在长文本生成任务中提升了 2.3-3.2% 的生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存压缩方法对预填充和解码阶段采用统一压缩策略，但压缩预填充缓存会破坏关键上下文信息；而解码阶段的压缩方法要么依赖固定的最近窗口，要么依赖瞬时注意力，无法捕捉 token 重要性的时序动态变化，导致重要 token 被过早驱逐或陈旧 token 被保留。

Method: 提出 Moment-KV，一种解码时 KV 缓存压缩方法。核心思想是将 token 重要性建模为连续演化的状态，通过带有衰减的注意力聚合（动量驱动的时间注意力聚合）同时捕捉 token 的长期影响和近期相关性。

Result: 在长文本生成任务中，相比现有方法提升 2.3-3.2% 的生成保真度，同时保持解码延迟不变。

Conclusion: 保留预填充缓存、仅压缩解码缓存的策略是有效的；动量驱动的时序注意力聚合比静态启发式方法更能准确建模 token 重要性动态变化。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache remains a major bottleneck for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in long-generation tasks. Prior work often applies uniform compression across both prefill and decoding caches, but compressing the prefill cache degrades performance by corrupting critical context. While preserving the prefill cache is essential, decoding-phase compression remains underexplored, with existing methods relying on rigid recency windows or instantaneous attention. Our analysis of attention dynamics reveals strong temporal patterns: critical tokens receive sustained attention over long horizons, while local reasoning involves short-lived bursts. Static heuristics fail to capture this behavior, leading to premature eviction of important tokens or retention of stale ones. We propose Moment-KV, a decoding-time KV cache compression method based on momentum-driven temporal attention aggregation. Our method models token importance as a continuously evolving state, where attention is aggregated with decay, capturing both long-term influence and recent relevance. Experiments show that Moment-KV significantly improves generation fidelity in long-generation tasks (2.3-3.2 %) while maintaining decoding latency.

</details>


### [196] [Redundant or Necessary? A Benchmark for Detecting Redundant Steps in Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29893)
*Minyang Hu,Bo Yang,Zhinuo Zhou,Jiachen Liang,Guo Jiahao,Yiyang Yin,Xiongwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并定义了智能体轨迹中的冗余步骤检测这一新研究领域，构建了包含标注轨迹的RedundancyBench基准，评估了三种检测方法，发现最佳方法仅达到24.88%的检测分数，揭示该任务的复杂性和挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估协议主要关注任务完成度，忽略了执行效率问题——智能体轨迹中常包含消耗大量资源但对任务完成贡献甚微的冗余步骤，亟需系统化的检测方法。

Method: 定义了冗余步骤检测问题；构建RedundancyBench基准，包含多样化任务及人工标注的轨迹（每步标记为冗余或必要）；开发并评估了三种代表性检测方法。

Result: 最佳方法在冗余步骤检测上仅达到24.88%的分数，部分方法表现不如随机猜测，表明当前方法在该任务上存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 冗余步骤检测是一项极具挑战性的任务，现有方法性能远未达到实用水平，亟需更多研究关注智能体执行效率问题。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, existing evaluation protocols primarily focus on task success, overlooking a critical aspect of agent behavior: execution efficiency. In practice, agent trajectories often contain redundant steps that consume substantial resources while contributing little to task completion. In this work, we propose and formulate a new research area: \textbf{redundant step detection} for agent trajectories. To support this initiative, we introduce \textbf{RedundancyBench}, a new benchmark that contains diverse tasks with carefully annotated trajectories, where each step is labeled according to its contribution to task completion. Using RedundancyBench, we develop and evaluate 3 representative methods to answer whether a step within trajectory is redundant or necessary. Our results show that even the best-performing method achieves only 24.88\% score in detecting redundant steps, while some methods perform worse than random guessing. These results highlight the task's complexity and the need for further research in this area. \footnote{Code and dataset in this paper are both available in \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RedundancyBench}{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RedundancyBench}.}

</details>


### [197] [Toward AI Systems That Understand Self and Others: A Multi-Phase Inference Framework for Human Cognitive Diversity and World-Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29930)
*Toru Takahashi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多相推理框架（MIM），形式化描述异构世界模型如何产生，并将世界模型对齐重新定义为使异构表征相互可处理，而非强制趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代社会的相互误解不仅源于价值观分歧，即便面对相同观测，不同主体也会形成不同的推理目标、状态表征、预测误差和更新优先级。现有方法缺乏对这种异构性的形式化描述。

Method: 提出多相推理框架，核心机制为多相推理机制（MIM），通过相形成空间、前景场、主体特定轮廓状态及状态表征间的对齐映射来形式化异构世界模型的产生。

Result: 将世界模型对齐重新定义为使异构表征可相互处理，而非强制达成单一价值体系的一致或趋同。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI系统提供了建设性词汇，通过使意义、价值和预测误差的差异变得可见、可比较和可转化，帮助人类理解自我与他人，并与哲学分歧、认知类型学、社会碎片化和AI对齐相连接。

Abstract: Mutual misunderstanding in contemporary society does not arise merely because people hold different opinions or values. Even under the same observations, different subjects may form different inferential targets, state representations, prediction errors, and update priorities. This paper proposes a multi-phase inference framework and defines its core internal mechanism as the Multi-Phase Inference Mechanism (MIM). MIM formalizes how heterogeneous world models arise through a phase-formation space, a foregrounding field, subject-specific profile states, and alignment maps between state representations. On this basis, the paper reframes world-model alignment as the problem of making heterogeneous representations mutually processable, rather than forcing agreement or convergence to a single value system. It further connects this formalism to philosophical disagreements, cognitive typology, social fragmentation, and AI alignment. The aim is to provide a constructive vocabulary for AI systems that can help humans understand self and others by making differences in meaning, value, and prediction error visible, comparable, and transformable.

</details>


### [198] [It`s All About Speed: AI`s Impact on Workflow in Music Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29931)
*Finn McClellan,Fabio Morreale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过民族志研究，探讨了AI和自动化工具对专业录音工程师、混音师和制作人音乐制作工作流程的影响，分析了用户与工具之间在速度、可控性和创意自主权方面的张力，并提出了缓解这些张力的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI和自动化工具在音乐制作领域的实际影响，了解专业从业者对这些工具的使用情况、态度以及面临的挑战，为工具设计提供指导。

Method: 采用民族志研究方法，聚焦于专业录音工程师、混音师和制作人，收集他们对常见AI和自动化软件的使用情况与看法。

Result: 发现了用户与自动化工具之间在速度与效率、可控性、维护创意自主权等方面的张力。

Conclusion: 这些张力可以通过优化工具设计来缓解，从而更好地满足专业用户的需求。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the results of an ethnographic study into the impact of AI and automated tools on music production workflow. Focusing specifically on professional participants who identified as recording engineers, mixers, and producers, we discuss their usage of common AI and automated software, as well as their sentiments on the proliferation of these tools. We discuss tensions that may be created between users and automated tools in key areas such as the need for speed and efficiency, controllability, and maintaining creative agency, and how these tensions may be alleviated through tool design.

</details>


### [199] [Make LLM Learn to Synthesize from Streaming Experiences through Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29940)
*Zhenlin Hu,Yan Wang,Zhen Bi,Zihao Xue,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang,Xiongtao Zhang,Zeyu Yang,Zhixuan Chu,Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StreamSynth设定（合成任务流式到达）和SynLearner框架（使模型能跨任务积累和迁移合成经验），实验证明该方法能有效利用历史任务经验提升后续任务合成性能，展现跨任务可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将数据合成视为孤立任务，忽视了模型能否从过往任务中积累经验并将其迁移到未来任务这一更根本的问题。

Method: 提出StreamSynth设定（任务流式到达）和SynLearner框架：鼓励模型探索多样合成模式、从反馈中学习、在任务演化中平衡样本质量与集合级多样性，从而实现可复用合成经验的获取。

Result: 多个基准上的实验表明SynLearner能有效利用早期任务经验提升后续任务的合成性能，展现一致的跨任务可迁移性。

Conclusion: 验证了StreamSynth设定的可行性，表明合成数据生成是一个经验驱动过程，能够从任务流中获益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely adopted for synthetic data generation, significantly reducing annotation costs. However, most existing studies treat synthesis as a set of isolated tasks and overlook a more fundamental question: whether a model can learn to synthesize by accumulating experience from past tasks and transferring it to future ones. In this work, we introduce StreamSynth, a new setting in which synthesis tasks arrive sequentially and experience from historical tasks provides informative signals for future synthesis. To address this setting, we propose SynLearner, a general framework that enables synthesis models to acquire reusable synthesis experience over a task stream. Instead of generating data independently for each task, SynLearner encourages the model to explore diverse synthesis patterns, learn from feedback, and balance sample quality with set-level diversity as tasks evolve. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that SynLearner effectively leverages experience from earlier tasks to improve synthesis performance on later ones, exhibiting consistent cross-task transferability. These findings provide evidence for the feasibility of StreamSynth and highlight synthetic data generation as an experience-driven process that can benefit from task streams.

</details>


### [200] [MuPHI: Learning Implicit Multimodal Harm Reasoning via Semantically Grounded Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29951)
*Anisha Saha,Varsha Suresh,Teodora Kamova,Sophia Wiedmann,Timothy Hospedales,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MuPHI数据集和MuPHIRM训练框架，用于提升多模态大语言模型在隐含有害内容检测与推理方面的能力，并通过推理导向的奖励优化实现更好的分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）擅长对表层感知线索进行字面推理，但在理解依赖隐式、上下文推理的有害语义方面存在不足，难以从表面良性图文对中识别出隐含的多模态有害内容。

Method: 构建MuPHI数据集（包含基于微妙多模态线索的有害图文对及其推理链标注）；提出MuPHIRM推理增强训练框架，通过多视角奖励优化学习联合语义表征。

Result: MuPHIRM在有害检测和推理质量上均取得提升，并在分布外泛化鲁棒性上优于既有训练基线和推理时基线方法。

Conclusion: 推理导向的奖励优化是构建超越基准捷径、实现良好泛化的多模态系统的有前景方向。

Abstract: Understanding how harm emerges from interaction between otherwise benign image-text pairs requires intent-aware cross-modal reasoning beyond surface-level features. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) excel at literal reasoning over perceptual cues but often fail to derive harmful semantics that rely on implicit, context-dependent reasoning. To evaluate VLMs on compositional harm detection and reasoning, we introduce Multimodal Pragmatic Harm Interpretation (MuPHI), a dataset containing image-text pairs where harm is encoded in subtle multimodal cues. MuPHI spans diverse harm categories and includes annotated harm rationales for assessing VLM reasoning chains. To improve both detection and reasoning in VLMs, we propose MuPHIRM, a reasoning-augmented training framework which learns joint semantics by optimizing multi-perspective rewards. MuPHIRM improves both harm detection and reasoning quality of VLMs while demonstrating superior out-of-distribution robustness compared to both trained and inference-time baselines. Our findings suggest that reasoning-oriented reward optimization offers a promising direction towards building multimodal systems that generalize beyond benchmark-specific shortcuts.

</details>


### [201] [Meta-Programming for Linear-time Temporal Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29965)
*Susana Hahn,Amade Nems,Javier Romero,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于元编程的灵活框架，通过扩展clingo的理论语法（引入类型规范和嵌套能力）并设计保护嵌套模态的转换流水线，统一实现了TEL、MEL、DEL等多种时序逻辑的语义，最终封装为metasp系统工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度优化的ASP系统固有的刚性阻碍了对不同逻辑设计的快速探索与实现，亟需一种灵活、统一的框架来支持多种时序逻辑的语义操作化。

Method: 通过扩展clingo的理论语法增加形式类型规范和嵌套能力，设计转换流水线在grounding阶段保护嵌套模态免受基于稳定模型的简化，实现TEL、MEL、DEL的元编码。

Result: 实现了TEL、MEL和DEL的元编码，提供了TEL的全面阐述，展示了管理MEL区间约束和DEL Fischer-Ladner闭包的关键特性，构建了metasp系统。

Conclusion: 所提出的元编程框架能够灵活地操作化多种时序逻辑语义，metasp系统封装了该工作流，为ASP时序扩展的快速原型设计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The development of temporal extensions of Answer Set Programming (ASP) has led to the emergence of non-monotonic linear-time (TEL), dynamic (DEL), and metric (MEL) temporal equilibrium logics. However, the inherent rigidity of highly optimized ASP systems often hinders the rapid exploration and implementation of alternative logical designs. In this work, we propose a flexible meta-programming framework that operationalizes the semantics of varied temporal logics through a unified, declarative framework. Our approach extends standard ASP meta-programming by augmenting clingo's theory grammar with formal type specifications and nesting capabilities. To ensure semantic correctness, we introduce a transformation pipeline that protects nested modalities from stable-model-based simplifications during grounding. We demonstrate the extensibility of our framework by implementing meta-encodings for TEL, MEL, and DEL. We provide a comprehensive account of TEL and highlight the key features for managing the interval constraints of MEL and the Fischer-Ladner closure in DEL. Finally, we introduce the metasp system, a versatile tool that encapsulates this workflow.

</details>


### [202] [Compass: Navigating Global Marine Lead Data Integration through Expert-Guided LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29966)
*Yiming Liu,Bin Lu,Meng Jin,Ziyuan Sang,Shuo Jiang,Lei Zhou,Xinbing Wang,Chenghu Zhou,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专家引导的LLM智能体框架Compass，通过知识树将复杂任务分解为可验证步骤，无需微调即可从超23万篇开放论文中提取海洋铅数据，构建了迄今最大的海洋铅数据库（3,751条记录），准确率达92%，并扩展了东海和南大洋等欠采样区域的覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋铅及其同位素是研究洋流和人为污染的关键示踪剂，但原位观测成本高且稀疏。大量历史数据埋藏在学术论文的非结构化内容中形成"数据孤岛"，人工提取不可扩展，而通用大语言模型因缺乏领域知识会产生幻觉和科学无效输出，亟需一种可规模化且科学严谨的数据提取方法。

Method: 采用专家引导的适配方法，使LLM无需微调即可执行严格的科学数据提取。具体实现为Compass智能体框架，其核心是与海洋科学家共同设计的知识树，将复杂任务分解为可验证步骤，引导智能体推理以确保科学有效性。

Result: 在超过23万篇相关开放获取论文中部署Compass，成功提取3,751条此前未收录的铅记录，构建了迄今最大的综合海洋铅数据库。通过多层验证和专家手动确认，准确率达92%。新增数据扩展了东海和南大洋等欠采样区域的覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 专家引导的智能体可以有效弥合通用LLM与高风险科学领域之间的差距，实现地球科学领域的可规模化数据发现。团队还发布了交互式可视化平台以促进开放科学访问。

Abstract: Marine lead (Pb) and its isotopes are critical tracers for ocean circulation and anthropogenic pollution, yet in-situ observations remain costly and sparse. While vast historical records exist, they lie buried within the unstructured content of academic papers, creating "data silos" inaccessible to comprehensive analysis. Manual extraction is unscalable, while general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) lack the necessary domain-specific knowledge, leading to hallucinations and scientifically invalid outputs. To address this, we introduce an expert-guided adaptation approach that enables LLMs to perform rigorous scientific data extraction without fine-tuning. We operationalize this approach through Compass, an LLM agent framework enhanced by a Knowledge Tree co-designed with marine scientists, which decomposes complex tasks into verifiable steps, guiding the agent's reasoning to ensure scientific validity. Deploying Compass across a corpus of over 230,000 relevant open-access papers, we successfully extract 3,751 previously unincorporated Pb records. This effort establishes the largest integrated marine Pb database to date. Beyond standard metrics, Compass demonstrates superior reliability through multi-layered validation, achieving 92% accuracy as confirmed through expert manual verification. The newly integrated data expand coverage in previously under-sampled regions such as the East China Sea and the Southern Ocean, providing an enriched data foundation for future scientific discoveries. We release an interactive visualization platform to facilitate open scientific access. Our work demonstrates that expert-guided agents can effectively bridge the gap between general-purpose LLMs and high-stakes scientific domains, enabling scalable data discovery in geosciences.

</details>


### [203] [Accelerating Constrained Decoding with Token Space Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29986)
*Michael Sullivan,Alexander Koller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CFGzip，一种离线压缩token搜索空间的技术，可将CFG约束解码引擎的延迟降低两个数量级，在复杂文法下实现最高7.5倍的总生成速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFG约束解码引擎在处理复杂上下文无关文法时，由于每一步需要对整个token词汇表进行搜索，导致计算开销过高，制约了其在复杂场景下的实用性。

Method: 提出CFGzip，一种离线压缩token搜索空间的技术，通过预计算和压缩有效token子集，减少解码引擎每一步的搜索范围。

Result: 与最先进文法引擎结合使用时，延迟降低达两个数量级，总约束生成时间加速最高达7.5倍。

Conclusion: CFGzip使得复杂CFG约束解码在大规模场景下变得可行，显著降低了CFG引擎的 overhead。

Abstract: To guarantee that an LLM's outputs conform to a specified structure, context-free grammar (CFG) decoding engines force the selection of next tokens that produce strings that conform to a given CFG. While current CFG-constrained decoding engines are highly optimized, the inherent costs arising from the massive per-step search space -- i.e. the entire token vocabulary -- result in intractably high overhead for more complex CFGs: precisely the situation where CFG engines are most useful. In this paper, we introduce CFGzip, an offline technique for compressing the token search space, which massively reduces CFG engine overhead. In experiments, we report latency reduction of up to two orders of magnitude when CFGzip is used with a SoTA grammar engine, yielding an up to 7.5x speedup in total constrained generation time: with CFGzip, constrained decoding is now feasible at scale for complex CFGs.

</details>


### [204] [KairosAgent: Agentic Time Series Forecasting with Fused Semantic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30002)
*Kun Feng,Ziwei Shan,Yuchen Fang,Yiyang Tan,Sihan Lu,Shuqi Gu,Lintao Ma,Xingyu Lu,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KairosAgent 是一个结合 LLM 推理器与 TSFM 预测器的智能体框架，通过动态调用分析工具、融合文本推理与数值预测，并利用强化学习进行多轮优化，实现跨域多模态时间序列的零样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在不足：TSFM 缺乏语义理解与未来导向的语义推理能力，LLM 则难以进行精确数值理解和定量预测。因此需要一种能同时整合精确数值理解、跨域语义理解和有效多模态融合的方法。

Method: 提出 KairosAgent 框架，包含：(1) LLM-based reasoner 和 TSFM-based forecaster 协同工作；(2) 动态调用分析工具增强 LLM 的数值理解和语义推理；(3) 将推理结果融合到 TSFM 流程中；(4) 构建大规模高质量轨迹语料库；(5) 引入多轮细化与回合级信用分配的强化学习范式。

Result: KairosAgent 在零样本预测任务上取得了优越性能，且能最大化利用预训练 LLM 和 TSFM 的效用。

Conclusion: KairosAgent 为高效且可解释的时间序列智能体提供了一个有前景的方向，证明了结合 LLM 推理与 TSFM 预测的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-domain multimodal time series forecasting is a challenging task, requiring models to integrate precise numerical comprehension, cross-domain semantic understanding, and effective multimodal fusion. Existing approaches either build Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) from scratch or leverage pretrained Large Language Models (LLMs). However, TSFMs often overlook semantic understanding and lack the ability to perform future-oriented semantic reasoning, and LLMs struggle with numerical comprehension and accurate quantitative forecasting. To overcome these limitations, we propose KairosAgent, a novel agentic framework for multimodal time series forecasting, including an LLM-based reasoner and a TSFM-based forecaster. KairosAgent unifies textual reasoning and numerical forecasting by dynamically invoking analytical tools to enhance the numerical understanding and semantic reasoning capabilities of LLMs. The reasoning results are subsequently fused into the TSFM pipeline, enabling more accurate and reliable future predictions. To further improve the reasoning, we curate a large-scale corpus of high-quality trajectories, alongside a reinforcement learning from forecasting paradigm with multi-turn refinement and turn-level credit assignment. Experiments demonstrate that KairosAgent achieves superior zero-shot forecasting performance while maximizing the utility of pretrained LLMs and TSFMs, presenting a promising direction for efficient and interpretable time series agents. The project page is at https://foundation-model-research.github.io/KairosAgent .

</details>


### [205] [From GPS Points to Travel Patterns: Flexible and Semantic Trajectory Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30014)
*Silin Zhou,Chenhao Wang,Yuntao Wen,Shuo Shang,Lisi Chen,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HTP，一种利用大语言模型分层生成旅行模式再生成GPS轨迹的方法，通过残差量化变分自编码器将微观轨迹压缩为宏观旅行模式令牌，并扩展LLM词表进行微调，在真实数据集上生成质量平均提升29.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹生成方法无法显式捕获出行模式，且只能在单一条件下生成固定长度轨迹，难以满足实际需求。

Method: 设计轨迹专用残差量化变分自编码器(RQ-VAE)，将微观GPS轨迹由粗到细量化压缩为宏观旅行模式令牌；扩展LLM词表加入旅行模式令牌，通过监督微调(SFT)使LLM对齐轨迹生成任务，实现多条件下的旅行模式序列生成，再解码为GPS点。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，HTP在生成质量上平均超越最强基线29.78%。

Conclusion: 分层生成旅行模式再生成GPS轨迹的方法有效提升了轨迹生成质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Urban trajectories play a crucial role in modeling urban dynamics and supporting various smart city applications. However, privacy concerns restrict access to large-scale and high-quality trajectory datasets. Trajectory generation provides a promising alternative by synthesizing realistic data to mitigate privacy risks. However, existing methods fail to explicitly capture travel patterns and can only generate fixed-length trajectories under a single condition. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HTP}, which \textbf{H}ierarchically generates \textbf{T}ravel patterns first and then generates GPS \textbf{P}oints by using large language models (LLMs), rather than directly generating GPS points. We first design a trajectory-specific residual quantization variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) that quantizes micro-level GPS trajectories into compact, macro-level travel pattern tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner. These tokens capture rich segment spatial irregularities, such as point density variations caused by traffic conditions. Then, we extend the LLM vocabulary with travel pattern tokens to align trajectory representations with the LLM input, and apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to align the LLM with the trajectory generation task, enabling generation of travel pattern sequences under various conditions. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets show that HTP outperforms the strongest baseline by an average of 29.78\% in terms of generation quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/slzhou-xy/HTP.

</details>


### [206] [RAISE: RAG Design as an Architecture Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30029)
*Zhen Chen,Yibing Liu,Weihao Xie,Yu Liang,Peilin Chen,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAG系统配置应形式化为架构搜索问题，并构建RAISE框架与基准，在7个数据集上评估13种搜索算法，实验表明优化性能高度任务依赖，无普遍最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的设计选择（查询重写、分块、检索深度、重排序、上下文压缩等）通常依赖启发式规则配置，缺乏系统化的评估与可复现性，阻碍了RAG流程的标准化研究。

Method: 将RAG配置问题形式化为RAG架构搜索（RAG Architecture Search），构建RAISE框架，实现13种搜索算法，在7个公开文本和多模态数据集上使用3个随机种子进行标准化评估。

Result: 优化性能高度任务依赖：同一方法在不同数据集上表现不一致，聚合排名不能作为普遍最优策略的证据。

Conclusion: RAISE为RAG超参数优化提供了公平、可复现、系统化的研究基准，揭示了当前方法缺乏跨任务泛化能力的问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems expose numerous design choices spanning query rewriting, chunking, retrieval depth, reranking, and context compression. In practice, these choices are often configured through heuristics, hindering systematic evaluation and reproducibility across settings. We argue that this challenge is best formulated as RAG architecture search. To support controlled and reproducible study of this problem, we introduce the RAG Intelligence Search Engine (RAISE), a comprehensive framework and benchmark for RAG hyperparameter optimization, which evaluates optimization methods for RAG pipelines under standardized search spaces and budgets. RAISE implements 13 search algorithms and evaluates them across seven public text and multimodal datasets using three random seeds. Our experiments show that optimization performance is highly task-dependent: methods that perform strongly on one dataset may not generalize consistently across others, cautioning against interpreting aggregate rankings as evidence of universally superior strategies. RAISE provides a common experimental substrate for fair, reproducible, and systematic research on RAG hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [207] [Teaching Values to Machines: Simulating Human-Like Behavior in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30036)
*Asaf Yehudai,Naama Rozen,Ariel Gera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过心理价值理论诱导LLMs产生类人价值观，并在超过500万道题的实验中验证了其与人类在价值结构和价值-行为关系上的一致性，表明价值诱导的LLMs可作为有效的心理模拟工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs虽能扮演不同角色，但尚不明确其行为是否能遵循连贯、类人的价值结构，因此作者引入心理学价值理论来系统性地探究这一问题。

Method: 基于已有的心理价值理论，使用经过验证的心理问卷，在领先的LLMs上进行超过500万道题的大规模实验，评估其价值结构及价值-行为关系，并与人类数据进行对比。

Result: 价值诱导的LLMs与人类在价值结构和价值-行为关系两个维度上均表现出高度一致性；引入人类价值分布可进一步提升群体模拟效果。

Conclusion: 价值诱导的LLMs能够作为有效的、基于心理学的模拟人类行为的工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate a remarkable capacity to adopt different personas and roles; however, it remains unclear whether they can manifest behavior that adheres to a coherent, human-like value structure. In this work, we draw on established psychological value theory to induce human-like values in LLMs and assess their alignment with patterns observed in human studies. Using validated psychological questionnaires, we conduct large-scale experiments -- over 5 million questions -- to evaluate value structures and value-behavior relationships in leading LLMs and compare them to humans. Our findings reveal strong agreement between value-prompted LLMs and humans across both dimensions. Moreover, incorporating human value distributions enhances population-level simulations with value-induced LLMs. These findings highlight the potential of value-induced LLMs as effective, psychologically grounded tools for simulating human behavior.

</details>


### [208] [Domain-Specific Data Synthesis for LLMs via Minimal Sufficient Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30039)
*Tong Ye,Hang Yu,Tengfei Ma,Xuhong Zhang,Jianguo Li,Peng Di,Peiyu Liu,Jianwei Yin,Wenhai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种归纳式领域特定数据合成框架DOMINO，通过参考样本学习最小充分领域表示来生成领域对齐的合成数据，在隐式领域定义的编程基准上提升Pass@1准确率最高达4.63%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据合成方法依赖演绎范式，需要显式的自然语言领域描述和精心设计的提示工程，在难以描述或正式阐述的现实领域场景中应用受限。本文旨在解决领域特征难以用自然语言表达时的数据合成问题。

Method: 提出DOMINO框架，通过对比解耦目标进行提示微调，从参考样本中学习最小充分领域表示，分离领域级模式与样本特定噪声，在保留核心领域特征的同时缓解过拟合。

Result: 在隐式领域定义的编程基准上，DOMINO合成的数据微调后Pass@1准确率相比强指令微调基线提升最高4.63%。理论证明了DOMINO扩展了合成数据分布的支持域，确保更大多样性。

Conclusion: 建立了一种新的归纳式领域特定数据合成范式，无需人工提示设计或自然语言领域规范即可实现实用且可扩展的领域自适应。

Abstract: Large Language Models have demonstrated remarkable progress in general-purpose capabilities and can achieve strong performance in specific domains through fine-tuning on domain-specific data. However, acquiring high-quality data for target domains remains a significant challenge. Existing data synthesis approaches follow a deductive paradigm, heavily relying on explicit domain descriptions expressed in natural language and careful prompt engineering, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios where domains are difficult to describe or formally articulate. In this work, we tackle the underexplored problem of domain-specific data synthesis through an inductive paradigm, where the target domain is defined only through a set of reference examples, particularly when domain characteristics are difficult to articulate in natural language. We propose a novel framework, DOMINO, that learns a minimal sufficient domain representation from reference samples and leverages it to guide the generation of domain-aligned synthetic data. DOMINO integrates prompt tuning with a contrastive disentanglement objective to separate domain-level patterns from sample-specific noise, mitigating overfitting while preserving core domain characteristics. Theoretically, we prove that DOMINO expands the support of the synthetic data distribution, ensuring greater diversity. Empirically, on challenging coding benchmarks where domain definitions are implicit, fine-tuning on data synthesized by DOMINO improves Pass@1 accuracy by up to 4.63\% over strong, instruction-tuned backbones, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness. This work establishes a new paradigm for domain-specific data synthesis, enabling practical and scalable domain adaptation without manual prompt design or natural language domain specifications.

</details>


### [209] [Learning to Choose: An Empowerment-Guided Multi-Agent System with semantic communication for Adaptive Method Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30042)
*Geremy Loachamín-Suntaxi,Robert Lazar,Dimitrios G. Giovanis,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Eleni D. Koronaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合上下文赌博机、结构化通信和语义检查点的多智能体框架，用于确保科学计算工作流中动作-结果因果归因的完整性，防止语义漂移，并通过敏感性分析和不确定性量化案例验证其在策略学习收敛性、鲁棒性和适应性上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体科学计算流水线中，智能体意图与执行动作之间的小不一致会导致语义漂移，使得最终执行过程偏离原始策略，破坏下游评估与自适应学习。现有方法能生成可执行代码，但缺乏保证跨智能体因果归因完整性的机制。

Method: 提出多智能体框架，整合（1）上下文赌博机用于自适应决策；（2）结构化智能体间通信；（3）语义检查点以保持动作-结果忠实性。集成专用LLM智能体、接地代码生成和自愈执行循环，并通过赋能（empowerment）视角进行理论解释。

Result: 在敏感性分析和不确定性量化案例中，未受检的语义漂移会退化策略学习；而所提框架在收敛性、鲁棒性和对新问题的适应性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 科学多智能体系统的设计原则：自适应决策必须与保证语义一致性和可靠信息流跨计算流水线的显式机制相结合。

Abstract: Automating scientific computing workflows requires more than generating executable code: autonomous systems must also select appropriate computational strategies, implement them faithfully, and ensure that the resulting outcomes remain causally attributable to the decisions that produced them. In multi-agent pipelines, this process is particularly fragile, as small inconsistencies between agent intentions and actions can lead to semantic drift, where the eventually executed procedure no longer reflects the originally selected strategy, thereby corrupting downstream evaluation and adaptation. In this work, motivated by the ATHENA framework (Toscano et al., 2025; Toscano et al., 2026) and the concept of empowerment (Yiu et al., 2025), we introduce a multi-agent framework that combines contextual bandits with structured inter-agent communication and, most importantly, semantic checkpoints that preserve action-outcome fidelity throughout the pipeline. The system integrates specialized large language model (LLM) agents, grounded code generation, and self-healing execution loops within an adaptive decision-making architecture. Interpreting the framework through the lens of empowerment, we show that reliable autonomous learning requires not only identifying high-quality actions, but also preserving the integrity of their propagation across agents. Using sensitivity analysis and uncertainty quantification workflows as representative case studies, we demonstrate that unchecked semantic drift degrades policy learning, whereas the proposed framework improves convergence, robustness, and adaptation to novel problem contexts. These results suggest a broader design principle for scientific multi-agent systems: adaptive decision-making must be coupled with explicit mechanisms that guarantee semantic consistency and reliable information flow across the computational pipeline.

</details>


### [210] [Robust and Generalizable Safety Steering for Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30049)
*Zihao Xue,Yan Wang,Zhen Bi,Long Ma,Zhonglong Zheng,Zeyu Yang,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang,Jie Xiao,Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeDIG提出了一种基于位置感知稀疏特征迁移的扩散Transformer安全引导框架，通过构建稀疏自编码器、鲁棒性预训练路由和混合安全操作，有效降低了目标域和整体的不安全生成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer的分层跨模态生成机制使得安全控制不同于简单的提示过滤或输出检测；有害语义可能弱表达于文本、逐步绑定到视觉隐空间、最终与渲染动态纠缠，导致固定层安全引导不稳定且已知风险的引导机制难以迁移到新风险域。

Method: 1) 在功能不同的DiT干预位置上构建稀疏自编码器(SAE)；2) 使用鲁棒性感知预训练路由，优先选择在源-目标风险偏移下保持稳定的干预点；3) 冻结SAE编码器作为可复用稀疏安全字典，仅适配解码器到目标域激活流形，分离可迁移安全特征与域特定激活几何；4) 推理时结合Blend(混合)和Repel(排斥)操作，将不安全激活转向安全流形或远离有害稀疏方向。

Result: 在FLUX.1 Dev和Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large上的实验表明，SafeDIG持续降低了目标域和整体的不安全生成率，同时保持了源域安全性和图像质量。

Conclusion: SafeDIG通过位置感知的稀疏特征迁移有效解决了扩散Transformer安全引导在风险域迁移下的不稳定性和不可迁移性问题，在保证源域安全性和图像质量的前提下提升了对目标风险的防御能力。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become a powerful backbone for text-to-image generation, but their layered and cross-modal generation process makes safety control fundamentally different from prompt-level filtering or output-level detection. Harmful semantics may be weakly expressed in text representations, progressively bound to visual latents, and finally entangled with rendering dynamics. As a result, safety steering at a fixed layer can be unstable, and a steering mechanism learned from known risks may not transfer reliably to a shifted target risk domain. We propose SafeDIG, a safety steering framework that formulates DiT safety adaptation as position-aware sparse feature transfer. SafeDIG first constructs Sparse Autoencoders over functionally distinct DiT intervention positions and uses robustness-aware pre-training routing to prioritize intervention sites that are expected to remain stable under source-target risk shift. It then separates transferable safety features from domain-specific activation geometry by freezing the SAE encoder as a reusable sparse safety dictionary and adapting only the decoder to the target-domain activation manifold. During inference, SafeDIG combines Blend and Repel operations to steer unsafe activations toward transferred safety manifolds or away from harmful sparse directions. Experiments on FLUX.1 Dev and Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large show that SafeDIG consistently reduces target-domain and overall unsafe generation rates while preserving source-domain safety and image quality.

</details>


### [211] [Selective QA over Conflicting Multi-Source Personal Memory: A Diagnostic Testbed and Method Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30087)
*Tiancheng Yang,Matthias Schonlau,Ilia Sucholutsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多源冲突的个人记忆，提出选择性问答基准（34,560个实例），评估各类方法在证据冲突或不完整时的表现，最佳融合解析器达到80.3%准确率，引入弃权机制后选择性准确率可达85.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法区分错误源于证据本身还是方法的冲突消解步骤，缺乏对多源冲突记忆场景下选择性QA的系统评估。

Method: 构建包含18个问题模板、8种推理类型、480个人设、4个随机种子、34,560个实例的基准，通过受控源失真和确定性真实标注，评估无源、单源、结构化融合方法及前沿LLM的表现。

Result: 最佳训练融合解析器准确率80.3%，最强纯提示LLM为70.0%；加入弃权后，融合解析器选择性准确率85.3%（覆盖率78.3%），最佳LLM为71.0%（覆盖率95.4%）。不同模型在不同推理类型上各有优劣。

Conclusion: 提出的基准能有效区分证据问题和冲突消解问题，融合方法在准确率上优于纯LLM，但LLM在覆盖率上更优；数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Emerging personal AI agents are moving toward persistent, multi-source memory. This creates an evaluation problem: systems must decide how to use conflicting or incomplete evidence; they cannot just retrieve facts from one clean history. Existing benchmarks rarely show whether an error came from the evidence given to a method or from the method's conflict-resolution step. We study this as selective QA over conflicting multi-source personal memory: systems answer based on conflicting, sometimes incomplete sources, or abstain when evidence is insufficient. We develop a benchmark containing 18 question templates across 8 reasoning types, 480 personas, 4 random seeds, and 34,560 instances, with controlled source distortions and deterministic ground truth. We evaluate the performance of baselines without access to any source, access to a single source, structured fusion methods, and frontier LLMs. The best trained fusion resolver reaches 80.3% accuracy, while the strongest prompt-only LLM baseline reaches 70.0%. With abstention, the same resolver reaches 85.3% selective accuracy at 78.3% coverage and the best LLM reaches 71.0% selective accuracy at 95.4% coverage. Different models have different strengths across reasoning types. We release the data, code, cached model outputs, and data-generating process for reuse.

</details>


### [212] [PokerSkill: LLMs Can Play Expert-Level Poker without Training or Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30094)
*Boning Li,Baoxiang Wang,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PokerSkill框架，通过将详细的基于规则的扑克技能作为LLM的结构化动作接口，无需训练和求解器即可在复杂不完美信息游戏中达到与基于数万核小时计算的系统相竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖基于反事实遗憾最小化的均衡求解器（需数百万核小时训练），要么LLM直接玩游戏性能远低于求解器，要么传统基于规则的智能体策略天花板较低。三者均存在明显局限，需要一种无需训练和求解器的高性能方法。

Method: 构建一个由人类扑克专家设计的层次化技能库，通过确定性上下文引擎分析当前状态并检索相关规则片段，约束LLM的行动选择到合理范围内，形成规则技能与LLM能力结合的框架。

Result: 在GTOWizard基准上，GPT-5.5 XHigh + PokerSkill达到-57±21 mbb/hand，Claude Opus 4.6达到-80±29 mbb/hand，Claude Opus 4.7达到-87±64 mbb/hand，相比默认提示基线减少49-61%损失，并优于强机器人Slumbot。

Conclusion: 规则技能本身不能构成强策略，LLM单独也无法玩好，但二者结合可产生无需训练和求解器、却能媲美基于数万核小时计算系统的智能体。这是首个LLM在复杂不完美信息游戏中无需游戏特定训练或求解器查询即达到竞争性能的演示。

Abstract: Poker is a landmark challenge for artificial intelligence. The dominant approach relies on equilibrium solvers built on counterfactual regret minimization, requiring millions of core-hours of training. Large Language Models (LLMs) possess extensive poker knowledge but perform far below solver-based agents when asked to play directly. Traditional rule-based poker agents are interpretable and training-free, but their strategic ceiling remains far below equilibrium play. We introduce \textbf{PokerSkill}, a training-free and solver-free framework that bridges this gap by using detailed rule-based poker skills as a structured action-grounding interface for LLMs. A deterministic context engine analyzes the current state and retrieves only the relevant fragments from a layered skill library, which is entirely designed by human poker experts, constraining the LLM's choice to reasonable actions. Against GTOWizard, a state-of-the-art GTO benchmark, GPT-5.5 XHigh with PokerSkill achieves $-57 \pm 21$ mbb/hand, Claude Opus 4.6 achieves $-80 \pm 29$ mbb/hand and Claude Opus 4.7 achieves $-87\pm 64$ mbb/hand, reducing losses by 49--61\% compared to default-prompt baselines and outperforming the strong bot Slumbot. Our key finding is that rule-based skills alone do not constitute a strong strategy, and LLMs alone cannot play well, but their combination yields an agent that requires neither training nor solver access yet competes with systems built on millions of core-hours of computation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an LLM achieving competitive performance in a complex imperfect-information game without game-specific training or solver queries. Code is available at https://github.com/lbn187/PokerSkill.

</details>


### [213] [VLA-Trace: Diagnosing Vision-Language-Action Models through Representation and Behavior Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30117)
*Haoyuan Shi,Xiancong Ren,Yingji Zhang,Qinfan Zhang,Jiayu Hu,Haozhe Shan,Han Dong,Jinpeng Lu,Yinda Chen,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VLA-Trace是一个渐进式诊断框架，通过统一证据链分析VLA模型的表征动态、因果控制归因和行为表现。实验发现π₀.₅和OpenVLA在微调动态、多模态路由策略和语义跟随能力上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解VLA模型如何将多模态知识转化为具身控制仍是一个开放挑战，现有方法缺乏系统性的诊断框架来追踪从表征到行为的完整证据链。

Method: 提出VLA-Trace框架，结合跨模态和检查点漂移中心核对齐(CKA)追踪表征演化、注意力敲除干预识别模态特定控制通路、以及展开级行为探针检测语义跟随和捷径依赖。

Result: 两种模型在VLA微调中表现出不同的模态特异性适应动态；依赖不同的多模态路由策略和层间依赖进行动作解码；VLA策略擅长视觉引导轨迹生成但细粒度语义跟随能力有限。

Conclusion: 指出了表征保持适应、因果VLA电路和组合语义控制等未来研究方向。

Abstract: Understanding how Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform multimodal knowledge into embodied control remains an open challenge. We present VLA-Trace, a progressive diagnostic framework that analyzes VLA models through a unified evidence chain from representation dynamics to causal control attribution and behavioral manifestation. It specifically combines cross-modal and checkpoint-drift centered kernel alignment (CKA) to trace representation evolution, attention knockout interventions to identify modality-specific control pathways, and rollout-level behavioral probes to examine grounding, shortcut dependence, and semantic following. Experiments on $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA reveal three key findings. First, the two models exhibit distinct modality-specific adaptation dynamics during VLA finetuning. Second, they rely on different multimodal routing strategies and layer-wise dependencies during action decoding. Third, although VLA policies excel at visually grounded trajectory generation, they remain limited in fine-grained semantic following. These findings highlight future directions for representation-preserving adaptation, causal VLA circuits, and compositional semantic control.

</details>


### [214] [Enhancing Multi-Agent Communication through Attention Steering with Context Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30136)
*Hongxiang Zhang,Yuan Tian,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-Radar，一种无需训练的上下文管理方法，通过时空衰减机制动态引导多智能体系统关注相关上下文，在五个基准测试中最高提升7.64个绝对百分点，且随智能体数量和交互轮次增加仍保持有效和鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在交互中会快速积累极长的对话历史，导致相关信息被无关上下文稀释，性能下降。

Method: 提出Agent-Radar，一种无需训练的上下文管理方法，通过新颖的时间与空间衰减机制动态引导每个智能体关注相关上下文。

Result: 在五个不同基准测试上超越现有方法，最高提升7.64个绝对百分点；随着智能体数量和交互轮次增加仍保持有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Agent-Radar的核心组件对性能至关重要，且在不同设置下具有可泛化性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have demonstrated remarkable performance on complex tasks through collaborative reasoning. However, these systems tend to rapidly accumulate extremely long conversation histories during interaction. As conversations lengthen, relevant information is increasingly diluted by irrelevant context, leading to degraded performance. In this work, we present Agent-Radar, a training-free context management method that dynamically steers each agent's attention toward relevant context with a novel temporal and spatial decay mechanism. Our experiments demonstrate that Agent-Radar outperforms state-of-the-art methods across five different benchmarks, yielding gains of up to 7.64 absolute points. Furthermore, our analysis shows that Agent-Radar remains effective and robust as the number of agents and interaction rounds increases. Finally, the ablation study shows that core components in Agent-Radar are crucial to performance and generalizable in different settings.

</details>


### [215] [Temporal Stability and Few-Shot Prompting in Math Task Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30151)
*Danielle S. Fox,Brenda L. Robles,Elizabeth DiPietro Brovey,Christian D. Schunn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过纵向实验评估了通用AI工具（Gemini）和教育专用AI工具（Coteach）利用任务分析指南（TAG）对数学任务认知需求进行分类的能力。研究发现：模型版本更新效果不一（Gemini稳定在58%，Coteach从75%降至50%），而少样本提示（few-shot prompting）能显著提升两者性能（Gemini升至67%，Coteach恢复至75%），表明提示工程技术比被动模型更新对专业化教育任务更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具在教育场景中的广泛应用，需要系统评估其分类数学任务认知需求的能力，并探究模型版本更新和提示工程技术对分类稳定性的影响，以指导教育工作者和研究者合理选择、评估和实施AI工具。

Method: 采用纵向研究设计，选取通用AI工具Gemini和教育专用AI工具Coteach，基于任务分析指南（TAG）对数学任务认知需求进行分类。实验分三个阶段：基线测试、模型版本更新后重测、以及使用少样本提示（每个认知需求类别提供两个示例任务）后再次测试。

Result: Gemini在版本更新后准确率稳定在58%，Coteach从75%下降至50%；引入少样本提示后，Gemini提升至67%，Coteach恢复至75%。

Conclusion: 提示工程技术比被动模型版本更新对专业化教育任务分类能力的影响更大且更可靠；模型版本更新并不总能带来性能提升；研究对教育场景中AI工具的选择、评估和实施具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: As AI tools become increasingly integrated into educational contexts, questions arise about both their stability over time and their responsiveness to prompt engineering techniques. This longitudinal study focused on different AI tools' ability to use the Task Analysis Guide (TAG; Stein \& Smith, 1998) to classify the cognitive demand of mathematics tasks. In particular, it examined whether this classification ability changed with (1) model version updates over time and (2) few-shot prompting using exemplar tasks. We tested a general-purpose AI tool (Gemini) and an education-specific AI tool (Coteach). The specific tools were selected because of their relatively high performance on relevant published benchmarks and prior task-specific tests. Models were tested at baseline, retested with model version updates, and then tested again using few-shot prompting (two exemplar tasks for each cognitive demand category). Results revealed that newer model versions alone produced mixed effects: Gemini's accuracy remained stable at 58\%, while Coteach's accuracy decreased from 75\% to 50\%. However, few-shot prompting improved both models' performance: Gemini increased to 67\% and Coteach recovered to 75\% accuracy. These findings demonstrate that prompt engineering techniques can have larger and more reliable effects than passive model improvements, and that version updates may not always improve performance on specialized educational tasks. The study has important implications for how educators and researchers should approach AI tool selection, evaluation, and implementation in educational contexts.

</details>


### [216] [BioRefusalAudit: Auditing Biosecurity Refusal Depth Using General and Domain-Fine-Tuned Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30162)
*Caleb DeLeeuw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在生物安全场景下的拒绝机制结构鲁棒性及内部激活特征，发现模型拒绝行为受提示格式、输出长度、文化法律因素影响显著，并引入散度分数D进行内部分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物安全评估通常只关注模型是否输出有害内容，忽略了拒绝行为本身是否结构稳健——即能否在提示框架、格式或输出长度的微小变化下保持不变。

Method: 跨5种架构测试模型对75个提示的拒绝行为，改变聊天模板格式和80词长度限制；引入散度分数D比较模型表面响应标签与稀疏自编码器特征激活；使用Schedule I但非毒性化合物测试过度拒绝。

Result: Gemma 2 2B-IT从未真正拒绝；Gemma 4 E2B-IT有模板时拒绝65/75，无模板时0/75；80词上限下两个Gemma模型拒绝率降至0%；Qwen 2.5 1.5B和Phi-3-mini过度拒绝83-87%良性内容；散度分数在Gemma 4上实现0.647点差距且零重叠。

Conclusion: 激活层面审计可能发现行为评估无法暴露的失败模式，且模型间差异巨大；拒绝行为更多追踪法律和文化显著性而非真实CBRN风险。

Abstract: Biosecurity evaluations of language models typically ask whether models produce hazardous output. This paper asks a complementary question: when a model refuses, is that refusal structurally sound, or does it disappear under modest changes to prompt framing, formatting, or output length? Across five architectures, no model cleanly discriminated benign from hazard. Gemma 2 2B-IT never genuinely refused across 75 prompts, hedging on every hazard-adjacent query. Gemma 4 E2B-IT refused 65/75 prompts with chat-template formatting and 0/75 without it. Both Gemma models collapsed to 0% under an 80-token cap. Qwen 2.5 1.5B and Phi-3-mini over-refused, flagging 83-87% of benign biology as hazardous. Llama 3.2 1B showed the only meaningful tier gradient (61-point spread). To probe what drives such over-refusal, we tested a panel of Schedule I but biologically non-toxic compounds (notably psilocybin cultivation, with FDA Breakthrough Therapy status). Some models refused these at rates exceeding genuinely hazardous biology, suggesting refusal tracks legality and cultural salience over CBRN hazard. To measure the internal side, we introduce a divergence score D comparing a model's surface response label to its internal sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature activations. Full D was computed on Gemma 2 2B-IT (Gemma Scope 1) and Gemma 4 E2B-IT (author-trained bio SAE). Two fine-tuned Gemma 2 domain SAEs were released. On Gemma 4, comply and refuse responses separated by a 0.647-point gap with zero overlap (n=75), though this is preliminary, with a narrow catalog, within-sample calibration, and Gemma-family-only SAE coverage. Built over one hackathon weekend on consumer hardware (GTX 1650 Ti Max-Q, plus Colab T4 for SAE training), this preliminary evidence suggests activation-level auditing may surface failure modes invisible to behavioral evaluation, with substantial variation across architectures.

</details>


### [217] [Double-Edged Sword or Sharp Tool? Designing and Evaluating Triadic LLM-Teacher Collaboration for K-12 Writing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30200)
*Canran Wang,Yuwen Yang,Zhen Wang,Ming Ma,Ding Yu,Chentai Wang,Keman Huang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）与教师、学生在K-12写作学习中的三方协作机制，通过开发一个三方协作系统，基于系统功能语言学构建多维评估框架及建议轨迹追踪流水线，收集了涵盖120所学校、10,195名学生、57,954篇作文的大规模实证数据。研究发现：LLM作为生成引擎缓解教师倦怠，教师作为教学把关人和桥梁保障反馈质量，两者对技能提升都至关重要，但存在语言过度扩展导致边际效用递减的"天花板效应"，提示应根据学生水平动态调整LLM-教师协作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型整合到K-12教育中是一把"双刃剑"，需要建立LLM、教师和学生三者之间的有效协作机制，但目前缺乏相应的系统设计和实证研究。

Method: 开发了三方协作系统支持K-12写作学习；基于系统功能语言学构建多维评估框架；设计建议轨迹追踪流水线；收集两年间120所学校10,195名学生的57,954篇作文进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 该系统通过战略性分工提升写作质量：LLM作为生成引擎缓解教师倦怠，教师作为教学把关人保障反馈质量；但发现语言过度扩展存在边际效用递减的天花板效应。

Conclusion: LLM和教师对技能提升都至关重要，但应随学生能力提高采用动态自适应的LLM-教师协作模式，以克服天花板效应。

Abstract: The double-edged sword of integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) requires an effective triadic collaboration mechanism among LLMs, teachers and students, especially for K-12 education. By developing a triadic collaboration system to support K-12 writing learning, a multidimensional evaluation framework grounded in Systemic Functional Linguistics and the suggestion trajectory tracing pipeline, this paper contributes a large-scale empirical dataset involving $57,954$ essays from $10,195$ students across $120$ schools over two years. Our findings confirm the efficacy of this system in improving writing quality through a strategic labor division: the LLM serves as a generative engine to mitigate teacher burnout, and the teacher acts as a pedagogical gatekeeper and bridge to guarantee feedback quality. While both LLM and teacher are critical for skill improvement, we uncover a ceiling effect where excessive linguistic expansion yields diminishing marginal utility. These suggest a dynamically adaptive LLM-teacher collaboration as student proficiency increases.

</details>


### [218] [When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219)
*Haoming Xu,Weihong Xu,Zongrui Li,Mengru Wang,Yunzhi Yao,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出**上下文信念管理（CBM）**任务，并引入**BeliefTrack**基准（涵盖规则发现与电路诊断），通过信念状态奖励的强化学习将模型失败率降低70.9%，表示级引导降低46.1%，揭示了LLM在长程交互中管理累积信息的三种失败模式：Failed Stay、Failed Update和Failed Isolation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程交互中语言模型需要管理不断累积的信息（何时更新、保留或忽略状态），但现有模型缺乏可测量的信念管理评估方法，因此需要建立形式化的基准和指标来诊断这一挑战。

Method: 提出CBM任务框架，构建BeliefTrack基准（封闭世界、有限信念空间、符号验证器支持逐轮评估），使用强化学习（信念状态奖励）和表示级引导两种方法进行干预。

Result: Vanilla LLMs在CBM任务上存在严重失败；显式信念追踪提示提升有限；强化学习（信念状态奖励）平均降低失败率70.9%；表示级引导降低失败率46.1%。

Conclusion: 信念管理是LLM长程交互的关键瓶颈，强化学习和表示级引导能有效缓解该问题，揭示了潜层信念状态动态与失败机制的关联。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions require language models to manage accumulating information: when to update their state, when to preserve their state, and what to ignore. We study this challenge as \textbf{Contextual Belief Management (CBM)}: maintaining a predicted belief state aligned with formal evidence while isolating task-irrelevant noise. To make CBM measurable, we introduce BeliefTrack, a closed-world benchmark spanning Rule Discovery and Circuit Diagnosis, where a finite belief space and symbolic verifiers enable exact turn-level evaluation. BeliefTrack diagnoses three failures: Failed Stay, Failed Update, and Failed Isolation. Across multiple LLMs, vanilla models exhibit severe CBM failures, while explicit belief-tracking prompts provide limited gains. In contrast, reinforcement learning with belief-state rewards reduces failure rates by 70.9\% on average. Further probing reveals latent belief-state dynamics behind these failures, and representation-level steering reduces failure rates by 46.1\% across two tasks\footnote{Code is coming soon at https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM.

</details>


### [219] [mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30283)
*Peter W. Rose,Benjamin M. Good,Amanda M. Saravia-Butler,Charlotte A. Nelson,James P. Balhoff,Yaphet Kebede,Patricia L. Whetzel,Christopher Bizon,Andrew I. Su,Sergio E. Baranzini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCP Server Proto-OKN 是一个基于 Python 的模型上下文协议服务器，通过自然语言使 AI 助手能够发现、检查、查询和集成科学知识图谱，降低生物医学和科学用户跨领域知识图谱分析的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低生物医学和科学用户跨领域知识图谱分析的门槛，使 AI 助手能够通过自然语言与科学知识图谱交互。

Method: 使用 Python 和 FastMCP 框架实现，提供图路由、模式检查、SPARQL 执行、本体扩展、多图查询和转录生成等功能。

Result: 实现了支持自然语言交互的 MCP 服务器，代码开源在 GitHub 仓库，并提供文档、客户端配置说明和示例分析转录。

Conclusion: mcp-proto-okn 通过标准化的协议接口，有效降低了 AI 助手集成和查询科学知识图谱的复杂度，特别适用于生物医学领域的跨图分析场景。

Abstract: MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users.
  mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [220] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个渐进式信息揭露的基准框架，用于评估LLM在科学发现与推理中的创新能力（低信息下）与严谨推理能力（全信息下），通过语义相似度与原子声明对比衡量假设与真实结论的偏差。在45篇论文上评估GPT-5、GPT-5.4、Gemini 2.5 pro、Gemini 3.1 pro，发现GPT-5.4在最小上下文下仍能保持0.7 F1的对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多集中于多跳检索的深度研究任务，但缺乏对LLM在科学发现中所需的创新推理能力的系统评估，特别是从原始问题到假设生成再到与真实结论对比的全流程测试。

Method: 构建渐进式信息揭露框架：模型初始仅接收论文主题与研究问题，随后逐步揭露技术细节；在每个阶段要求模型生成假设，通过将假设拆解为原子声明，与原始论文结论进行自动语义相似度计算，衡量语义偏离程度。评估维度包括最低信息下的创新性和完整信息下的严谨推理能力。

Result: 在45篇论文（涵盖生物活性材料、机械材料、纳米材料）上评估四个模型，GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro性能优于前代，GPT-5.4在最小上下文条件下仍能保持0.7 F1的对齐度。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统评估LLM的科学推理与发现能力提供了基础，对下一代AI科学家/协同科学家系统的开发至关重要。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test.
